# External Pine Corpus Output Reference-Free Audit v1

Date: 2026-09-11
Measurement commit: `7090a75da11025053a2ad491a6ec07a0ec864ce5`
Machine-readable companion: `reports/external-pine-corpus-output-reference-free-v1.json`

This pass intentionally uses no member property map and no value oracle. It re-executes the 1961 scripts that already produced output in the current v5, v6, and v7 daily-profile reports, then inspects only the emitted visible numeric plot payloads for all-NaN and constant finite series.

## Headline

Reference-free audit completed for 1961/1961 produced-output rows with 0 rerun failures. It found 1159 all-NaN plot fields across 263 scripts and 945 constant finite plot fields across 238 scripts.

The property-map pass is deliberately not included here. `pine-member-property-map-v2.json` is the single source for rule-driven checks, but fires from rules not yet exercised against known-good outputs must stay tagged as leads until source-read validation promotes them.

V7 comparison retained for the record: v5+v6 were 2000 mostly random scripts and left 1 real current gap between them; v7 was 456 targeted scripts and yielded 19 real gaps before routing.

| Corpus | Produced-output rows |
| --- | --- |
| v5 | 872 |
| v6 | 796 |
| v7 | 293 |

## Rerun Failures

None.

## Method

- Source reports are the current daily produced-output denominators: v5 872, v6 796, v7 293.
- Re-execution uses `createStandardCorpusBars()` from the external corpus runner: 1600 volatile daily bars with varied close and volume.
- Audited plot payloads are visible numeric `plot`, `plotbar`, and `plotcandle` fields only. Fills, hlines, bgcolor/barcolor, plotshape, plotchar, and plotarrow are excluded from this first pass because static masks, levels, colors, and sparse event markers are common intentional outputs.
- All-NaN fires when every sampled value in an audited visible plot field is `na`/null.
- Constant finite fires when at least 1500 finite values in an audited plot field are identical over the volatile 1600-bar input.
